Tap to send each car into the loop without crashing. Plan your entry points and keep the traffic flowing smoothly.</div><button type="button" class="buttonReset m82lRUpJLGRrwux8_eBE">Show more<span role="img" aria-hidden="true" style="--icon-src: url('https://a.poki-cdn.com/icons/ui-masks/ui-icons-plus.svg');" class="kzPaMiKIK5c1hyP3aSiu AfaGcDcSKpA3JuDfG9Ye"></span></button></div></div><div class="aYbRzQy1dkv5k0ahUrTx"><p>
Car Circle is a traffic puzzle game where you control a stream of cars and guide them into a roundabout without causing a pile-up. Tap to send each car into the flow at just the right moment, keep the traffic moving smoothly, and beat the clock before time runs out. It sounds simple, but the faster the cars come, the harder it gets to keep everything clean. One wrong tap and the whole thing falls apart. How long can you keep the traffic flowing?</p>

<h3>How to play Car Circle?</h3>
<p>
Click or tap to play.</p>
